HF16 | One finite field for all values of this same frame | proved_under_exact_declared_background_and_supplied_data_hypotheses
One finite Galois field contains every normalized unramified value of the constructed frame, with shifts 0,1/2,0,0,0. Containment of its primitive generator in LL0 gives ValLL0 uniformly for every later assembly and then ValLL.

HF17 | Construct actual Base with finite field requirements simultaneously | proved_under_exact_declared_background_and_supplied_data_hypotheses
